FILE run_verification.py
"""Run the unmodified existing suite with scratch confined to this directory."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import time

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
TMP = OUT / "tmp"
TMP.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
env = {**os.environ, "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "PYTHONUTF8": "1",
       "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8", "TMP": str(TMP), "TEMP": str(TMP),
       "TMPDIR": str(TMP)}

def git(*args):
    result = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
    return {"code": result.returncode, "stdout": result.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace"),
            "stderr": result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace")}

files = ["src/app.py", "src/query.py", "web/try.html", "tests/test_app.py",
         "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"]
before = {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT / p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in files}
(OUT / "verification_initial.json").write_text(json.dumps({"head": git("rev-parse", "HEAD"),
    "status": git("status", "--short"), "hashes": before}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "tests", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider"]
start = time.perf_counter()
run = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, env=env, capture_output=True)
(OUT / "pytest.txt").write_bytes(run.stdout + run.stderr)
after = {p: hashlib.sha256((ROOT / p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in files}
summary = {"command": "python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider",
           "environment": {k: env[k] for k in ("PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE", "PYTHONUTF8", "PYTHONIOENCODING", "TMP", "TEMP", "TMPDIR")},
           "exit_code": run.returncode, "wall_seconds": round(time.perf_counter() - start, 3),
           "hashes_unchanged": before == after, "hashes_after": after}
(OUT / "pytest_summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
print((run.stdout + run.stderr).decode("utf-8", "replace"))
print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
raise SystemExit(run.returncode)


FILE run_http.py
"""Round 5 HTTP and QA regression checks; all output stays beside this file."""
import ast
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import types
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
PORT = 8880
BASE = f"http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}"
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
os.environ["PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE"] = "1"
DB = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"

def git(*args):
    p = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True)
    return {"code": p.returncode, "stdout": p.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace"),
            "stderr": p.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace")}

def post(path, payload):
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    try:
        res = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        res = e
    return {"status": res.status, "body": json.loads(res.read())}

before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
initial = {"head": git("rev-parse", "HEAD"), "status": git("status", "--short"),
           "db_sha256": before}
(OUT / "initial_integrity.json").write_text(json.dumps(initial, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
with socket.socket() as s:
    assert s.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0, "audit port already occupied"

log = (OUT / "server.txt").open("w", encoding="utf-8")
proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", "-u", "src/app.py", str(PORT)], cwd=ROOT,
                        env=os.environ.copy(), stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
try:
    for _ in range(100):
        if proc.poll() is not None:
            raise RuntimeError("server exited")
        try:
            urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
            break
        except OSError:
            time.sleep(.1)
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("server unavailable")
    # Re-run every original HTTP case without executing a writer in m1.
    original = (ROOT / "audit_evidence_claims/m1/http_audit.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    adapted = original.replace("8876", str(PORT)).replace("bfcb83d", "514929c")
    (OUT / "http_audit.py").write_text(adapted, encoding="utf-8")
    run = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(OUT / "http_audit.py")], cwd=ROOT,
                         capture_output=True, env={**os.environ, "PYTHONIOENCODING": "utf-8"})
    (OUT / "http_stdout.txt").write_bytes(run.stdout + run.stderr)
    assert run.returncode == 0

    regression = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", str(OUT / "regression_http.py")],
        cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, env={**os.environ, "PYTHONIOENCODING":"utf-8"})
    (OUT / "regression_http_stdout.txt").write_bytes(regression.stdout + regression.stderr)
    assert regression.returncode == 0

    # Load the previous linker/answer module in memory; use the identical database.
    old = types.ModuleType("m2b_old_query")
    old.__file__ = str(ROOT / "src/query.py")
    sys.modules[old.__name__] = old
    code = subprocess.run(["git", "show", "bfcb83d:src/query.py"], cwd=ROOT,
                          capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode("utf-8")
    exec(compile(code, old.__file__, "exec"), old.__dict__)
    baseline = old.OncoKG()
    baseline.conn.execute("PRAGMA query_only=ON")
    html = (ROOT / "web/try.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    questions = json.loads(re.search(r"const EX2 = (\[.*?\]);", html, re.S).group(1))
    # Verify CLI/dashboard demos are covered by the page list, without running
    # their entrypoints (which write tracked report/dashboard artifacts).
    sources = {}
    for filename, variable in [("src/query.py", "questions"),
                               ("src/make_dashboard_data.py", "demo_questions")]:
        parsed = ast.parse((ROOT / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assignments = [n for n in ast.walk(parsed) if isinstance(n, ast.Assign)
                       and any(isinstance(t, ast.Name) and t.id == variable for t in n.targets)]
        value = assignments[-1].value
        if isinstance(value, ast.BoolOp):
            value = value.values[-1]
        sources[filename] = ast.literal_eval(value)
        assert set(sources[filename]) <= set(questions)
    answers = []
    for question in questions:
        previous = baseline.answer(question, use_llm=False).to_dict()
        current = post("/api/ask", {"text": question})
        answers.append({"question": question, "http_status": current["status"],
                        "identical_to_bfcb83d": current["body"] == previous,
                        "current": current["body"], "previous": previous})
    baseline.conn.close()
    (OUT / "http_demo_results.json").write_text(json.dumps({"source_lists": sources,
        "answers": answers}, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"demo_questions": len(answers),
        "all_http_200": all(a["http_status"] == 200 for a in answers),
        "all_identical_to_bfcb83d": all(a["identical_to_bfcb83d"] for a in answers)}))
finally:
    proc.terminate()
    proc.wait(timeout=10)
    log.close()
    with socket.socket() as s:
        port_closed = s.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", PORT)) != 0
    after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    (OUT / "http_lifecycle.json").write_text(json.dumps({"pid": proc.pid, "port": PORT,
        "server_exit": proc.returncode, "port_closed": port_closed,
        "database_unchanged": after == before, "db_sha256_before": before,
        "db_sha256_after": after}, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


FILE perf_probe.py
"""Bounded subprocess benchmarks; writes confined to m5 and never calls a model."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import types

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))

if len(sys.argv) > 1:
    import query
    import textrules as T
    mode, kind, size = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3])
    module = query
    if mode == 'm4':
        module = types.ModuleType('perf_m4_query')
        module.__file__ = str(ROOT / 'src/query.py')
        sys.modules[module.__name__] = module
        code = subprocess.run(['git', 'show', 'bf436c6:src/query.py'], cwd=ROOT,
                              capture_output=True, check=True).stdout.decode('utf-8')
        exec(compile(code, module.__file__, 'exec'), module.__dict__)
    kg = module.OncoKG()
    kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
    text = {'plain': ' '.join(['x'] * size),
            'drug': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['osimertinib']),
            'split': ';'.join(['x'] * size),
            'single': 'x' * size,
            'late_qualifier': ' '.join(['x'] * size + ['biosimilar', 'bevacizumab'])}[kind]
    calls = {'is_distinct_product': 0, 'is_radiotracer': 0, 'characters': 0}
    for name in ['is_distinct_product', 'is_radiotracer']:
        original = getattr(T, name)
        def wrapped(value, name=name, original=original):
            calls[name] += 1
            calls['characters'] += len(value)
            return original(value)
        setattr(T, name, wrapped)
    start = time.perf_counter()
    entities = kg.link_entities(text)
    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
    kg.conn.close()
    print(json.dumps({'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'characters': len(text),
                     'seconds': elapsed, 'calls': calls, 'entities': entities}))
else:
    env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
           'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'),
           'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMPDIR': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
    cases = [('plain', n) for n in [50, 100, 200, 250, 500, 1000]]
    cases += [('drug', 244), ('split', 250), ('single', 500), ('late_qualifier', 235)]
    rows = []
    for kind, size in cases:
        for mode in ['m4', 'HEAD']:
            try:
                proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-B', __file__, mode, kind, str(size)],
                                      cwd=ROOT, env=env, capture_output=True, timeout=20)
                row = json.loads(proc.stdout) if proc.returncode == 0 else {
                    'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'error': proc.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')}
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                row = {'mode': mode, 'kind': kind, 'size': size, 'timeout_seconds': 20}
            rows.append(row)
            (OUT / 'perf_results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
            print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)


FILE perf_check_http.py
"""Bounded probe of the answer field passed back into the new linker."""
import concurrent.futures
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8880'
DB = ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'

def post(path, payload, timeout):
    started = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
        try:
            response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout)
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
            response = error
        return {'status': response.status, 'body': json.loads(response.read()),
                'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
    except TimeoutError:
        return {'timeout_seconds': timeout, 'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}

with socket.socket() as sock:
    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8880)) != 0
before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1'}
with (OUT / 'perf_check_server.txt').open('w', encoding='utf8') as log:
    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'src/app.py', '8880'], cwd=ROOT,
        env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
    try:
        for _ in range(100):
            assert proc.poll() is None
            try:
                urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
                break
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(.1)
        else:
            raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
        normal = post('/api/explore', {'text':'EGFR'}, 5)
        small = post('/api/check', {'text':'EGFR', 'answer':' '.join(['x']*100)}, 5)
        payload = {'text':'EGFR', 'answer':' '.join(['x']*500)}
        slow = post('/api/check', payload, 5)
        started = time.perf_counter()
        get = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=2)
        get.read()
        live_get = {'status': get.status, 'seconds': time.perf_counter()-started}
        follower = post('/api/explore', {'text':'EGFR'}, 2)
        output = {'normal_before': normal, 'small_check_199_characters': small,
                  'slow_check_input': payload, 'slow_check_999_characters': slow,
                  'get_while_check_running': live_get, 'normal_after_check_timeout': follower,
                  'limitation':'Client timeouts bound the observation; not a completed latency or permanent deadlock claim.'}
        (OUT / 'perf_check_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2), encoding='utf8')
        print(json.dumps({k: ({x:y for x,y in v.items() if x!='body'} if isinstance(v,dict) else v)
                          for k,v in output.items() if k!='slow_check_input'}))
    finally:
        proc.terminate()
        proc.wait(timeout=10)
        with socket.socket() as sock:
            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 8880)) != 0
        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        (OUT / 'perf_check_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid':proc.pid, 'port':8880,
            'port_closed':closed,'database_unchanged':before==after,'server_exit':proc.returncode},indent=2),encoding='utf8')
        assert closed and before==after


FILE m5_http.py
"""Round-5 independent HTTP confirmations and bounded latency/lock measurements."""
import concurrent.futures
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
PORT = 8880
BASE = f'http://127.0.0.1:{PORT}'
DB = ROOT / 'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite'

def post(path, text):
    start = time.perf_counter()
    request = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=json.dumps({'text': text}).encode(),
                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        response = error
    raw = response.read()
    return {'path': path, 'text': text, 'characters': len(text), 'status': response.status,
            'seconds': time.perf_counter() - start, 'bytes': len(raw), 'body': json.loads(raw)}

with socket.socket() as sock:
    assert sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0, 'audit port occupied'
before = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
env = {**os.environ, 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE': '1', 'PYTHONUTF8': '1',
       'PYTHONIOENCODING': 'utf-8', 'TEMP': str(OUT / 'tmp'), 'TMP': str(OUT / 'tmp')}
with (OUT / 'm5_server.txt').open('w', encoding='utf-8') as log:
    proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-B', '-u', 'src/app.py', str(PORT)],
                            cwd=ROOT, env=env, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                            creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
    try:
        for _ in range(100):
            assert proc.poll() is None
            try:
                urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=1).read()
                break
            except OSError:
                time.sleep(.1)
        else:
            raise RuntimeError('server unavailable')
        inputs = [
            'bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib',
            'bevacizumab - awwb/ osimertinib',
            'bevacizumab-awwb / osimertinib',
            'bevacizumab - awwb / osimertinib',
            'bevacizumab-awwb| osimertinib',
            'bevacizumab-awwb; osimertinib',
            'bevacizumab-awwb and osimertinib',
            'liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin',
            'liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin',
            'liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin',
            'biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab',
            'Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
            'Erlotinib; hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
            'Erlotinib and hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab',
            'Erlotinib hydrochloride', 'erlotinib',
            'Pegylated interferon alfa-2a', 'PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A',
            'CHEMBL1201560', 'EGFR biosimilar bevacizumab',
            'AstraZeneca biosimilar bevacizumab',
            'osimertinib\nbiosimilar bevacizumab',
        ]
        cases = [post(path, value) for value in inputs
                 for path in ['/api/explore', '/api/resolve']]
        for value in [
            'Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
            'Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib?',
            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and nanoparticle formulations of doxorubicin?',
            'Which trials evaluate liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin?',
            'Which trials evaluate biosimilar and investigational version of bevacizumab?',
            'Which trials evaluate Erlotinib, hydrochloride biosimilar bevacizumab?',
            'Which trials evaluate Pegylated interferon alfa-2a?',
            'Which trials evaluate PEGINTERFERON ALFA-2A?',
            'Which trials evaluate osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
            'Which companies sponsor osimertinib and biosimilar bevacizumab?',
        ]:
            cases.append(post('/api/ask', value))
        (OUT / 'm5_http.json').write_text(json.dumps({'base': BASE, 'cases': cases},
            ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
        for case in cases:
            body = case['body']
            if case['path'] != '/api/resolve':
                print(json.dumps({'text': case['text'], 'path': case['path'],
                    'center': body.get('center', {}).get('id'), 'refused': body.get('refused'),
                    'also': body.get('also'), 'evidence_count': len(body.get('evidence', [])),
                    'first_edge': (body.get('evidence') or [{}])[0].get('edge_id')},
                    ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)
        slow = ' '.join(['x'] * 250)
        sequential = []
        for path, text in [('/api/explore', 'EGFR'), ('/api/explore', slow),
                           ('/api/ask', slow), ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*244+['osimertinib'])),
                           ('/api/explore', ' '.join(['x']*251))]:
            sequential.append(post(path, text))
        # Four simultaneous allowed 499-character requests contend for the actual engine lock.
        barrier = threading.Barrier(4)
        def send_slow(_):
            barrier.wait()
            return post('/api/explore', slow)
        started = time.perf_counter()
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
            concurrent_rows = list(pool.map(send_slow, range(4)))
        elapsed = time.perf_counter()-started
        # Readable normal request behind one long lookup; .1 s is a measured scheduling gap,
        # not a claim that a specific internal lock acquisition has been observed.
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            future = pool.submit(post, '/api/explore', slow)
            time.sleep(.1)
            follower = post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')
            leader = future.result()
        result = {'sequential': sequential, 'concurrent': concurrent_rows,
                  'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow': {'leader': leader, 'follower': follower},
                  'normal_after': post('/api/explore', 'EGFR')}
        (OUT / 'perf_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
        print(json.dumps({'performance_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in sequential],
                          'concurrent_seconds': [r['seconds'] for r in concurrent_rows],
                          'concurrent_wall_seconds': elapsed, 'normal_behind_slow_seconds': follower['seconds']}))
    finally:
        proc.terminate()
        proc.wait(timeout=10)
        with socket.socket() as sock:
            closed = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', PORT)) != 0
        after = hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        (OUT / 'm5_http_lifecycle.json').write_text(json.dumps({'pid': proc.pid, 'port': PORT,
            'server_exit': proc.returncode, 'port_closed': closed, 'database_unchanged': before == after,
            'db_sha256_before': before, 'db_sha256_after': after}, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
        assert closed and before == after

FILE http_audit.py
"""Read-only HTTP probes for 514929c. Writes only next to this script."""
import concurrent.futures
import hashlib
import json
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:8880"
DB = ROOT / "data/processed/oncokg.sqlite"


def request(payload=None, raw=None, path="/api/explore"):
    data = raw if raw is not None else json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False).encode()
    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=data,
                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    start = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as err:
        response = err
    content = response.read()
    try:
        body = json.loads(content)
    except ValueError:
        body = content.decode(errors="replace")
    return {"status": response.status, "bytes": len(content),
            "ms": round((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000, 3), "body": body}


def digest():
    return hashlib.sha256(DB.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


before = digest()
get = urllib.request.urlopen(BASE, timeout=10)
html = get.read()
(OUT / "served_try.html").write_bytes(html)

"""Independent live-server verification of name-linking regression candidates."""
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:8880"

def post(path, text):
    data = json.dumps({"text": text}, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
    request = urllib.request.Request(BASE + path, data=data,
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    start = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        response = urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=30)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
        response = error
    return {"path": path, "input": {"text": text}, "status": response.status,
            "elapsed_ms": round((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000, 3),
            "body": json.loads(response.read())}

"""Copy prior audit entrypoints; only output plumbing changes, all writes in m6."""
import hashlib, json, os, subprocess, sys
from pathlib import Path
OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
names = ['run_verification.py','run_http.py','regression_http.py','supplemental_http.py',
         'attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py','perf_check_http.py','perf_probe.py']
manifest = []
for name in names:
    source = ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m5'/name
    original = source.read_text(encoding='utf8')
    adapted = original.replace('8880','8886')
    if name == 'run_http.py':
        adapted = adapted.replace('replace("bfcb83d", "514929c")','replace("bfcb83d", "b7a00f9")')
    (OUT/name).write_text(adapted,encoding='utf8')
    manifest.append({'source':str(source.relative_to(ROOT)), 'copy':str((OUT/name).relative_to(ROOT)),
                     'source_sha256':hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                     'changes':'port 8880->8886; HTTP audit commit annotation updated where present'})
(OUT/'root_reuse_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
tracked = subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'],cwd=ROOT).decode('utf8').split('\0')
hashes = {p:hashlib.sha256((ROOT/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in tracked if p and (ROOT/p).is_file()}
(OUT/'tracked_before.json').write_text(json.dumps({'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),'hashes':hashes},indent=2),encoding='utf8')
print(json.dumps({'copied':len(names),'tracked_hashed':len(hashes)}))

+"""Execute inherited reproducers, owning and closing one HTTP port at a time."""
+import json,os,subprocess,sys,time
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'root_checks'
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+env={**os.environ,'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PYTHONUTF8':'1','PYTHONIOENCODING':'utf-8',
+     'TMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TEMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMPDIR':str(OUT/'tmp')}
+names=(['run_verification.py'] if 'tests' in sys.argv else ['perf_probe.py'] if 'perf' in sys.argv
+       else ['run_http.py','supplemental_http.py','attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py','perf_check_http.py'])
+rows=[]
+for name in names:
+    start=time.perf_counter()
+    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/name)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
+    (OUT/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    rows.append({'script':name,'exit_code':p.returncode,'seconds':time.perf_counter()-start})
+    (OUT/('execution_'+sys.argv[-1]+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')

+"""Root-owned replay copies, isolated from concurrently appearing root m6 artifacts."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib, json
+
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'root_checks'
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+(OUT/'tmp').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+names=['run_verification.py','run_http.py','http_audit.py','regression_http.py',
+       'supplemental_http.py','attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py',
+       'perf_check_http.py','perf_probe.py']
+manifest=[]
+for name in names:
+    p=ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m5'/name
+    code=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+    code=code.replace('OUT.parents[1]','OUT.parents[2]').replace('8880','8896')
+    if name=='run_http.py':
+        code=code.replace('(OUT / "http_audit.py").write_text(adapted, encoding="utf-8")',
+                          '(OUT / "http_audit.py").write_text(adapted.replace("OUT.parents[1]", "OUT.parents[2]"), encoding="utf-8")')
+    (OUT/name).write_text(code,encoding='utf-8')
+    manifest.append({'source':str(p.relative_to(ROOT)), 'copy':str((OUT/name).relative_to(ROOT)),
+                     'source_sha256':hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                     'adaptations':'root depth and port only; dynamic HTTP copy root depth'})
+(OUT/'reuse_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+print('Prepared',len(manifest),'copies')

src/textrules.py
260: 
261: # A placebo arm must never create a "this company administered this drug" edge.
262: RE_PLACEBO = re.compile(r"\bplacebo\b|\bsham\b|\bvehicle\s+control\b", re.I)
263: 
264: # Combination separators, longest first so "combined with" wins over "with".
265: RE_COMBO = re.compile(
266:     r"\s*(?:\+|&|,|、|，|/(?=\s*[A-Za-z])|\bcombined\s+with\b|"
267:     r"\bin\s+combination\s+with\b|\bcombination\s+with\b|\bplus\b|\band\b|\bor\b|"
268:     r"\bfollowed\s+by\b)\s*",
269:     re.I,
270: )
271: 
272: RE_PAREN = re.compile(r"\(([^()]*)\)|\[([^\[\]]*)\]")
273: 
274: # Development codes ("AZD9291", "BMS-986504", "PF-07224826", "TSC-204-A0201").
275: # One character apart is a different molecule, so these must never be matched
276: # approximately -- the usual string-similarity intuition is wrong here.
277: RE_DEV_CODE = re.compile(
278:     r"^[A-Za-z]{1,5}[\s\-]?\d{2,8}(?:[\s\-][A-Za-z0-9]{1,8})*$")
279: 
280: # Trailing lot/format suffixes that do not change the substance: "BMS-936558-01"
281: RE_DEV_CODE_SUFFIX = re.compile(r"^(?P<core>[A-Za-z]{1,5}[\s\-]?\d{3,8})[\s\-]0?\d{1,2}$")
282: RE_MULTISPACE = re.compile(r"\s+")
283: RE_PUNCT_EDGE = re.compile(r"^[\s\-–—,;:.、，。]+|[\s\-–—,;:.、，。]+$")
284: 
285: 
286: # Isotope labels on radiotracers: 18F, 68Ga, 99mTc, 177Lu, 64Cu, 89Zr, 131I.
287: # These look exactly like development codes but name an isotope, not a molecule,
288: # and the substance they label is usually not in a therapeutic vocabulary at all.
289: RE_ISOTOPE = re.compile(r"^\[?(\d{1,3}m?)[\s\-]?([A-Za-z]{1,2})\]?$")
290: ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS = {
291:     "f", "c", "n", "o", "ga", "cu", "zr", "i", "lu", "tc", "in", "y", "at",
292:     "ac", "pb", "re", "sm", "sr", "ra", "rb", "br", "h", "p", "s",
293: }
294: 
295: 
296: def is_isotope_label(text: str) -> bool:
297:     m = RE_ISOTOPE.match((text or "").strip())
298:     return bool(m and m.group(2).lower() in ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS)
299: 
300: 
301: # "[18F]F-AraG", "68Ga-DOTATATE": an isotope prefix marks the whole string as a
302: # radiolabelled tracer, whose unlabelled moiety is not the administered drug.
303: RE_RADIOTRACER = re.compile(
304:     r"(?:^|[\s\(\[])\[?\d{1,3}m?[\s\-]?[A-Za-z]{1,2}\]?\s*[-–]\s*\w"
305:     r"|(?:^|[\s\(])\[\d{1,3}m?[\s\-]?[A-Za-z]{1,2}\]\s*[A-Za-z]", re.I)
306: 
307: 
308: def is_radiotracer(text: str) -> bool:
309:     t = (text or "").strip()
310:     m = RE_RADIOTRACER.search(t)
311:     if not m:
312:         return False
313:     head = re.match(r"^[\s\(\[]*\[?(\d{1,3})m?[\s\-]?([A-Za-z]{1,2})\]?", t)
314:     return bool(head and head.group(2).lower() in ISOTOPE_ELEMENTS)
315: 
316: 
317: def is_dev_code(text: str) -> bool:
318:     """True for alphanumeric development codes, where fuzzy matching is unsafe."""
319:     t = (text or "").strip()
320:     return bool(RE_DEV_CODE.match(t))
321: 
322: 
323: def dev_code_core(text: str) -> str | None:
324:     """Strip a trailing lot/format number: 'BMS-936558-01' -> 'BMS-936558'."""
325:     m = RE_DEV_CODE_SUFFIX.match((text or "").strip())
326:     return m.group("core") if m else None
327: 
328: 
329: def _strip_words(text: str, words: list[str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:
330:     fired = False
331:     for w in sorted(words, key=len, reverse=True):
332:         pattern = re.compile(rf"\b{re.escape(w)}\b", re.I)
333:         if pattern.search(text):
334:             text = pattern.sub(" ", text)
335:             fired = True
336:     return text, fired
337: 
338: 
339: def canonical_key(text: str) -> str:
340:     """Aggressive key used for dictionary lookup (never shown to a user)."""
341:     t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "")
342:     t = "".join(c for c in t if not unicodedata.combining(c))
343:     t = t.lower()
344:     t = t.replace("’", "'").replace("‐", "-").replace("–", "-").replace("—", "-")
345:     t = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "", t)
346:     return t
347:

+"""Execute inherited reproducers, owning and closing one HTTP port at a time."""
+import json,os,subprocess,sys,time
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'root_checks'
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+env={**os.environ,'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PYTHONUTF8':'1','PYTHONIOENCODING':'utf-8',
+     'TMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TEMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMPDIR':str(OUT/'tmp')}
+names=(['run_verification.py'] if 'tests' in sys.argv else ['perf_probe.py'] if 'perf' in sys.argv
+       else ['run_http.py','supplemental_http.py','attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py','perf_check_http.py'])
+rows=[]
+for name in names:
+    start=time.perf_counter()
+    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/name)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
+    (OUT/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    rows.append({'script':name,'exit_code':p.returncode,'seconds':time.perf_counter()-start})
+    (OUT/('execution_'+sys.argv[-1]+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
+    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

## Residual blocker: internal slash/pipe is never separated

`bevacizumab - awwb/osimertinib` selects **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, with 122 trials and no refused item. The same string passed to the existing resolver produces `bevacizumab - awwb: BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` and `osimertinib: EXACT / CHEMBL3353410`. This is an internally established refused product, without any external pharmacology inference. The precise input already exists in the m5 attack and is still failing in the fresh replay. `bevacizumab - awwb|osimertinib` similarly leaks. [Current exact input/results](new_focused.json), [reused m5 script and results](names_m5_attack.json).

Cause: `src/query.py:143-154` splits punctuation only for commas/semicolons, strips slash/pipe only from the **edges** of a whitespace token, and adds boundaries only when that raw token starts or ends with slash/pipe. An internal `awwb/osimertinib` is unchanged. `_qualified` (`99-110`) never gets a run ending in `awwb`; the anchored suffix regex (`src/textrules.py:156-157,213-223`) misses. Positive matching (`src/query.py:212-237`) then accepts the separate `bevacizumab` token. Required outcome: refuse that product and either retain the separate osimertinib or fail closed; never draw CHEMBL1201583.

Related unchanged earlier probes include `bevacizumab - awwb&osimertinib`, `bevacizumab - awwb& osimertinib`, `bevacizumab-awwb+ osimertinib`, and Arabic comma/semicolon, halfwidth comma or fullwidth slash appended to `awwb`. These all draw BEVACIZUMAB in [m5 confirmation replay](names_m5_confirm.json). The explicit ASCII slash/pipe contract alone is sufficient to block approval; these are additional normalization gaps under the same mechanism, not independent findings.

## Alias and demo consequences of current item design

All seven page QA demos retain evidence counts **12, 12, 11, 1, 12, 0, 0**. Six complete results equal bfcb83d; Mvasi differs only in refusal metadata. [Demo comparison](names_demo_results.json).

The 7,511 unique alias-key sweep shows 57 formerly linked lone aliases now have no drug hit against the exact bf436c6 functions: the recorded pegylated alias plus **56 aliases containing newly enforced list punctuation/words**. Examples include `ICI 182,780`, `CP-675,206`, `Nk cells, autologous`, `Anusol plus hc`. The list-boundary design explains these losses; no demo uses them, and this report does not relabel these deliberate boundary restrictions as originator leaks. [Summary](names_m5_summary.json).

However, five complete stored aliases now select only a *different* drug ID, which is a separate identity consequence for the parent reviewer to assess under the explicit item policy:

| Stored complete alias | Unique stored drug | Selected current drug |
|---|---|---|
| `Usp mab 003, monoclonal igg1` | DROZITUMAB / CHEMBL1743008 | PEMBROLIZUMAB / CHEMBL3137343, matched `mab` |
| `CP-358,774` | ERLOTINIB / CHEMBL553 | ERLOTINIB HYDROCHLORIDE / CHEMBL1079742, matched `CP-358` |
| `Rapamycin, 42-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-` | EVEROLIMUS / CHEMBL1908360 | SIROLIMUS / CHEMBL413, matched `Rapamycin` |
| `12,13-desoxyepothilone b, cis` | EPOTHILONE D / CHEMBL96172 | PRALSETINIB / CHEMBL4582651, matched `cis` |
| `ENDOSTATIN, N-TERMINAL-MGGSHHHHH` (stored preferred name) | CHEMBL4297946 | ENDOSTATIN / CHEMBL4297747 |

These are reproducible in `alias_anomalies` of [the reused attack](names_m5_attack.json); each row contains stored alias provenance, current and predecessor hits, and resolver output. No claim of new false graph edges is made. They arise because punctuation-bearing chemical aliases are interpreted as separate list items before the full stored identity can be considered (`src/query.py:143,216-217,277`).

## Fresh blocker independently confirmed

**`bevacizumab - awwb/osimertinib` draws BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583 with 122 trials and no refusal.** The same Engine's resolver splits it into `bevacizumab - awwb: BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` and `osimertinib: EXACT / CHEMBL3353410`. `bevacizumab - awwb/foo` has the same originator leak. The control `bevacizumab - awwb/ osimertinib` correctly refuses the qualified first component and selects only osimertinib. [Reproducer](interior_separator_probe.py), [raw results](interior_separator_probe.json).

`src/query.py:143-154` handles slash/pipe only at the ends of a whitespace token. Interior `awwb/osimertinib` survives unsplit; the anchored suffix guard misses it, and the accepted bare `bevacizumab` token at `212-236` becomes the originator. This violates the current explicit glued-separator design. `bevacizumab - awwb|osimertinib` also leaks, although the resolver itself likewise misses the pipe, so the slash case is the strongest independent refusal comparison. The primary report supplies actual server HTTP and QA confirmation.

## Limits, integrity and reproduction

UI scripts execute the current entire page script in a Node VM with controlled DOM/fetch stubs. This verifies generated markup, event handlers, asynchronous sequencing and output data, not browser paint/layout or browser-executed XSS. The current stored data contain no self-loops or unknown node types; the latter uses a synthetic UI fixture. Positive/negative Infinity is a controlled failure, not a drawn wrong value. No application model call was made; `Engine.ask` uses `use_llm=False` (`src/app.py:180-181`). The application itself opens SQLite read/write, so read-only guarantees here are path/integrity evidence, not a global SQLite permission guarantee.

Replay commands are preserved in [fixture_runs.json](fixture_runs.json) and [ui_runs.json](ui_runs.json). Additional commands:

```text
python -B audit_evidence_claims/m6/db_ui/db_recheck.py
python -B audit_evidence_claims/m6/db_ui/db_assertions.py
node audit_evidence_claims/m6/db_ui/ui_missing_sponsor_count.js
python -B audit_evidence_claims/m6/db_ui/ui_assertions.py
python -B audit_evidence_claims/m6/db_ui/interior_separator_probe.py
```

**Standards axis: 0 documented violations**, separate from the originator-leak specification blocker. [Standards review](standards_findings.md).

"C:\\WINDOWS\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command '$env:PYTHONUTF8='"'1'; "'$env:PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='"'1'; @'
import ast,json
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('audit_evidence_claims/m6/confirm_http.py')
s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
t=ast.parse(s)
a=next(n for n in ast.walk(t) if isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and any(isinstance(x,ast.Name) and x.id=='values' for x in n.targets))
lines=s.splitlines()
values=['ENDOSTATIN, N-TERMINAL-MGGSHHHHH','CHEMBL4297946','Rapamycin, 42-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-','CP-358,774','Usp mab 003, monoclonal igg1','12,13-desoxyepothilone b, cis','Erlotinib/hydrochloride','Erlotinib|hydrochloride','biosimilar anastrozole/Arimidex','osimertinib/EGFR']
lines[a.lineno-1:a.end_lineno]=['        values='+repr(values)]

+"""End-to-end linker/check cost under HTTP's input and answer character caps."""
+import json,statistics,sys,time
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'root_checks'
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'src'))
+from app import Engine
+e=Engine();e.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+cases=[('plain_'+str(n),' '.join(['x']*n)) for n in (250,500,1000,2000)]
+cases += [('split_2000',';'.join(['x']*2000)),('many_matched',';'.join(['osimertinib']*333)),
+          ('many_refused',';'.join(['Mvasi bevacizumab']*235)),
+          ('many_joiners',' and '.join(['x']*666))]
+rows=[]
+for name,value in cases:
+    times=[]
+    for _ in range(3):
+        start=time.perf_counter(); ents=e.kg.link_entities(value); times.append(time.perf_counter()-start)
+    start=time.perf_counter(); result=e.check('EGFR',value); check=time.perf_counter()-start
+    rows.append({'name':name,'text':value,'characters':len(value),'link_seconds':times,
+                 'link_median_seconds':statistics.median(times),'check_seconds':check,
+                 'entity_count':len(ents),'evidence_count':result['evidence_count']})
+e.kg.conn.close()
+(OUT/'cost_stress.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps([{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ('text','link_seconds')} for r in rows],indent=2))

@@ -0,0 +1,18 @@
+"""Execute inherited reproducers, owning and closing one HTTP port at a time."""
+import json,os,subprocess,sys,time
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'root_checks'
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+env={**os.environ,'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PYTHONUTF8':'1','PYTHONIOENCODING':'utf-8',
+     'TMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TEMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMPDIR':str(OUT/'tmp')}
+names=(['run_verification.py'] if 'tests' in sys.argv else ['perf_probe.py'] if 'perf' in sys.argv
+       else ['run_http.py','supplemental_http.py','attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py','perf_check_http.py'])
+rows=[]
+for name in names:
+    start=time.perf_counter()
+    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/name)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
+    (OUT/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    rows.append({'script':name,'exit_code':p.returncode,'seconds':time.perf_counter()-start})
+    (OUT/('execution_'+sys.argv[-1]+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
+    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

+"""Execute inherited reproducers, owning and closing one HTTP port at a time."""
+import json,os,subprocess,sys,time
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'root_checks'
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+env={**os.environ,'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PYTHONUTF8':'1','PYTHONIOENCODING':'utf-8',
+     'TMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TEMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMPDIR':str(OUT/'tmp')}
+names=(['run_verification.py'] if 'tests' in sys.argv else ['perf_probe.py'] if 'perf' in sys.argv
+       else ['run_http.py','supplemental_http.py','attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py','perf_check_http.py'])
+rows=[]
+for name in names:
+    start=time.perf_counter()
+    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/name)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
+    (OUT/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    rows.append({'script':name,'exit_code':p.returncode,'seconds':time.perf_counter()-start})
+    (OUT/('execution_'+sys.argv[-1]+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
+    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

# Round 6 independent boundary attack

Current HEAD: `b7a00f9`. Reproducer: `python -B audit_evidence_claims/m6/attacks/fresh_probe.py`.
Evidence: [106 exact inputs, full linker/resolver/map metadata and eight QA responses](fresh_results.json),
[compact per-input comparison](fresh_summary.json). The predecessor column transplants the exact
`link_entities` and `_drug_spans` methods from `514929c` into a separate Engine, with unchanged current
dictionary and helper functions. Both database connections are replaced with SQLite `mode=ro` connections.

## M6-B1: interior glued separator hides the refused suffix (blocker)

Exact input: `bevacizumab - awwb/osimertinib`.

* Current map: `BEVACIZUMAB`, `CHEMBL1201583`, **122 trials**, no refusal.
* Current full resolver: `bevacizumab - awwb` is `BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT`, while `osimertinib` is
  `EXACT / CHEMBL3353410`.
* Exact QA `Which trials evaluate bevacizumab - awwb/osimertinib?` returns **12** originator rows;
  first edge `NCT00955305|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201583|NCT00955305`.
* Exact QA `Which companies sponsor bevacizumab - awwb/osimertinib?` returns **12** originator rows;
  first edge `ORG:roche|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF|CHEMBL1201583|`.
* `bevacizumab - awwb|osimertinib` also draws BEVACIZUMAB. The pipe resolver does not split that form,
  so the slash form is the strongest exact resolver-versus-linker reproduction.
* Free separator control `bevacizumab - awwb / osimertinib` correctly retains only OSIMERTINIB and
  discloses refusal. The bare suffix is also refused.

**Cause:** `src/query.py:143-154` splits commas, semicolons and whitespace, strips slash/pipe only from
token edges, and tests only `startswith`/`endswith`. A slash inside `awwb/osimertinib` is left inside
one token. The anchored suffix check at `src/textrules.py:156-157,213` therefore misses the product;
the standalone `bevacizumab` token then links at `src/query.py:221-236`. No whole-item guard can
refuse a suffix that it never recognizes. `Engine.explore` selects that positive hit at `src/app.py:88-95`.

This contradicts the explicit glued-separator design and the refusal approval bar. **Inherited:** the
same exact input also maps to CHEMBL1201583 with the `514929c` predecessor; the latest repair did not
cover a separator glued on both sides.

Related consequences of the same splitting gap:

| Exact input | Current result | Boundary contract consequence |
|---|---|---|
| `osimertinib/EGFR` | `not_found` | Neither separate named entity is found. |
| `biosimilar anastrozole/Arimidex` | Entire string refused, no centre | Valid Arimidex alone after the declared separator is lost; full resolver accepts Arimidex. |
| `Erlotinib/hydrochloride` | `CHEMBL1079742`, **31 trials** | A salt is assembled across the declared separator; full resolver keeps first-item parent `CHEMBL553`. |
| `Erlotinib|hydrochloride` | `CHEMBL1079742`, **31 trials** | Same identity construction across pipe. |

These related observations are not separate blockers and do not allege that any listed stored trial count
is itself wrong. The wrong identity is selected. All are inherited in the exact predecessor comparison.

## M6-B2: punctuation normalization defeats the anchored guard (blocker)

Exact input: `bevacizumab-awwb。` (final character U+3002 IDEOGRAPHIC FULL STOP).

* Full resolver: component `bevacizumab-awwb`, `BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT`, no accepted drug.
* Map: `BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583`, **122 trials**, no refusal, matched alias `Bevacizumab awwb`.
* `Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb。?` returns **12** originator rows, first
  `NCT00955305|EVALUATES|CHEMBL1201583|NCT00955305`.
* `Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb。?` returns **12** originator rows, first
  `ORG:roche|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF|CHEMBL1201583|`.
* `bevacizumab-awwb.` (ASCII full stop) and bare `bevacizumab-awwb` refuse correctly.
* The same resolver/linker disagreement occurs for `bevacizumab-awwb—` (trailing U+2014),
  `—bevacizumab-awwb`, `bevacizumab-awwb-`, and `-bevacizumab-awwb`.

**Cause:** the token-edge strip set at `src/query.py:147` differs from the resolver's `RE_PUNCT_EDGE`
at `src/textrules.py:283`, applied by `clean_surface` at `382-383`. The suffix guard is anchored to
both ends (`156-157`), so extra punctuation hides the suffix. Positive alias matching then calls
`canonical_key` (`339-346`), which removes the very punctuation that caused refusal to fail, recovering
the stored originator alias. The whole-item/eight-word scans at `src/query.py:99-110` cannot repair
this because every scanned run preserves the offending punctuation.

**Inherited:** the exact `514929c` linker also draws CHEMBL1201583 for U+3002. This remains a blocker
despite not being newly introduced.

Additional variants `(bevacizumab-awwb)`, `[bevacizumab-awwb]`, quoted `"bevacizumab-awwb"`, U+2010
or U+2014 internal hyphens, and quoted `"[89Zr] trastuzumab"` also draw their originators. For these
the full resolver itself also accepts the originator, so they are recorded as shared guard/normalization
limitations, not presented as exact resolver/linker disagreements. The U+3002 reproduction is sufficient
without an external pharmacology assumption.

## Controls and limits

* All **16** suffix/tracer/liposomal/generic-version tests with 0, 4, 8 or 16 filler words on each side
  refuse. No new eight-word-window escape was demonstrated.
* `liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin`, `liposomal and sustained release doxorubicin`,
  and intervening `EGFR`/extra conjunction controls refuse the qualified drug.
* `Mvasi and bevacizumab`, `(Mvasi) and bevacizumab`, `Mvasi® and bevacizumab`, and `Abraxane and paclitaxel`
  retain the separately named originator and disclose the product refusal.
* Same-item fail-closed over-refusals and the known pegylated alias limitation are not new findings.
* Exploratory reversed-description inputs, such as `doxorubicin in nanoparticle and liposomal formulations`,
  are in the raw evidence. They show the limitation of forward-only carry, but are not promoted as a
  separate finding under the user's explicitly stated segmentation/carry policy.
* Actual HTTP reproductions are owned by the primary reviewer; this file reports independent in-process
  results. No application code or tracked file was edited.

+**Cause:** `src/query.py:143-154` splits comma/semicolon and whitespace, strips `/` and `|` only from the edges of a token, and creates boundaries only for `startswith`/`endswith`. Interior `awwb/osimertinib` remains one token. `_qualified` (`99-110`) never checks a run ending at `awwb`, so the anchored suffix guard (`src/textrules.py:156-157,213-223`) misses it. +### M6-B2 — P1: edge punctuation is removed for alias matching after defeating refusal
+
+Exact input **`bevacizumab-awwb。`**, with final **U+3002 IDEOGRAPHIC FULL STOP**, returns HTTP 200 and **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, matched alias `Bevacizumab awwb`, and no refusal. The same `/api/resolve` input yields a single `bevacizumab-awwb: BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` component with no accepted drug. [HTTP](root_checks/confirm_http.json).
+
+`Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb。?` and `Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb。?` each return **12 originator evidence rows**, with the same first trial/sponsor edges as B1. ASCII-period `bevacizumab-awwb.` and bare `bevacizumab-awwb` are refused; their trial/sponsor questions have **0 evidence**. Trailing U+2014, trailing ASCII hyphen and leading ASCII hyphen also produce the resolver/linker disagreement over HTTP.
+
+**Cause:** the strip set at `src/query.py:147` differs from the resolver's edge-punctuation normalization (`src/textrules.py:283,382-383`). The suffix regex is anchored at both ends (`156-157`), so the retained punctuation defeats it. The later `canonical_key` call (`src/query.py:221`; `src/textrules.py:339-346`) removes that punctuation and recovers an alias for the originator. Whole-item and eight-word checks preserve the same offending character, so increasing their coverage cannot fix this mismatch.
+
+**Status:** inherited under `514929c`, still a blocker. Quoted/bracketed suffix variants and some Unicode hyphens also fail both resolver and linker; those are explicitly distinguished from the exact U+3002 resolver disagreement in [independent findings](attacks/FINDINGS.md). No external pharmacology assumption is needed for the primary reproduction.
+
+**Required outcome:** normalization that enables a positive alias must not remove evidence that prevented its refusal guard from matching. The U+3002 and hyphen-edge controls must refuse the product before mapping or retrieving originator evidence.
+

+These are qualified descriptions of the preceding drug, not two separately named drugs. [Actual HTTP](root_checks/carry_http.json), [independent current/predecessor comparison](attacks/reversed_results.json).
 
-### M6-B1 — P1: an internal glued separator still hides a refused suffix
+`Which trials evaluate doxorubicin (nanoparticle and liposomal formulation)?` returns **3** originator rows, first `NCT01970540|EVALUATES|CHEMBL53463|NCT01970540`. Its sponsor question returns 3 rows, first `ORG:imunon|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF|CHEMBL53463|`. The corresponding two bevacizumab questions return **12** originator rows each, beginning with B1's trial/sponsor edges.
 
-Exact unchanged-server request:
+**Cause:** `src/query.py:167-174` splits on `and` even inside the parenthetical qualifier. The drug is in the earlier, unqualified item. The later `liposomal formulation)` or `biosimilar)` item has no drug, so `192-197` stores it as `dangling` and continues. Carry is applied only to a **subsequent** drug (`183-184,199-201`); it never retracts the already accepted earlier drug. The final qualifier can therefore disappear without even an output Refused entry. The full resolver preserves the original product context for its guard (`src/normalize.py:707,729-735`).
 
-```text
-POST /api/explore
-{"text":"bevacizumab - awwb/osimertinib"}
-```
+Controls `doxorubicin (liposomal formulation)` and the original M5-B2 `liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin` both refuse and produce **0 QA evidence**. Separate-drug controls `osimertinib and liposomal doxorubicin` and `doxorubicin and liposomal paclitaxel` retain the independently named first drug. [HTTP controls](root_checks/carry_http.json).
 
-HTTP 200 returns **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583, 122 graph trials**, `matched_text:'bevacizumab'`, `also:[]`, no refusal. The identical `/api/resolve` request returns two components:
+**Status:** inherited under `514929c`; the latest forward-carry repair does not solve this design failure. The stated carry direction explains the implementation but does not waive the approval bar against a refused product becoming its originator. This is an **under-refusal**, not the accepted same-item over-refusal or M5-S2.
 
-```text
-bevacizumab - awwb : BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT, drug_id null
-osimertinib        : EXACT, CHEMBL3353410
-```
+**Required outcome:** preserve the qualifier's scope within an unambiguously single product description, or refuse that ambiguous product as a whole. Do not propagate every trailing qualifier backwards indiscriminately across genuinely separate named products.
 
-This is a repository-internal contradiction about an explicitly refused product, not a pharmacology assumption. The actual page form produces BEVACIZUMAB header/SVG/trail and **61 retained relationship rows**, with no refusal disclosure. [Full HTTP responses](confirm_http.json), [actual page-script rendering](ui/boundary_render_results.json).

+The following tables include prior should-fix items and qualifications, not only blockers. FIXED refers to the cited reproduction; it does not certify the broader guarantee contradicted above. Detailed raw cases and source references are also in the [names matrix](names/REPORT.md) and [DB/UI matrix](db_ui/REPORT.md).
 
-**Cause, with a discriminating control:** `src/query.py:143-154` splits commas and whitespace, strips `/|` only at token edges, and increments clauses only for starts/ends. It never splits internal `awwb/osimertinib`. Thus no checked word run exposes the anchored `bevacizumab - awwb` suffix (`src/textrules.py:156-157,213-223`); later acceptance links the standalone parent (`src/query.py:221-237`). `bevacizumab - awwb / osimertinib` and the previously reported `bevacizumab-awwb/ osimertinib` now correctly select only OSIMERTINIB with refusal. Changing only separator placement changes safety. An eight-word window cannot repair a separator inside a token.
+### M1
 
-`bevacizumab - awwb/EGFR` and `bevacizumab - awwb|osimertinib` repeat the map leak. For the pipe case the full resolver also lacks the split; do not interpret it as a resolver disagreement. Attached `+`/`&` variants from the earlier M5 evidence remain unsafe. In `bevacizumab-awwb+ osimertinib`, the anchored guard misses the trailing plus, then `canonical_key` removes it and retrieves stored `Bevacizumab awwb` as the originator alias (`src/textrules.py:339-346`). Actual HTTP trial/sponsor QA again uses BEVACIZUMAB. [Old-variant replay](names/names_m5_confirm.json), [HTTP](confirm_http.json).

+## Should-fix and design consequences, separate from blockers
+
+### M6-S1 — P2: complete stored names containing list punctuation can select a different drug
+
+The strict item policy can do more than refuse a lone alias: six complete stored aliases in the reused 7,511-key sweep now select only another drug ID. Examples confirmed over actual HTTP:
+
+| Complete stored name / alias | Stored unique identity | Current selected identity |
+|---|---|---|
+| `ENDOSTATIN, N-TERMINAL-MGGSHHHHH` — preferred name | CHEMBL4297946 | ENDOSTATIN / CHEMBL4297747 |
+| `Rapamycin, 42-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-` | EVEROLIMUS / CHEMBL1908360 | SIROLIMUS / CHEMBL413 |
+| `CP-358,774` | ERLOTINIB / CHEMBL553 | ERLOTINIB HYDROCHLORIDE / CHEMBL1079742 |
 
-The full-string `is_distinct_product` is true for both; each phrase names one drug in a qualified formulation. `and` joins formulation descriptions rather than two separately named drugs. Both are inherited in the exact M5-method comparator. [Direct predicate/comparison](names/focused_new.json), [actual HTTP, including sponsor QA](confirm_http.json).
+[HTTP and QA](root_checks/alias_http.json), [all six aliases, stored provenance and previous/current hits](names/names_m5_attack.json), [summary table](names/REPORT.md). The other examples are `Usp mab 002, monoclonal igg1`, `Usp mab 003, monoclonal igg1`, and `12,13-desoxyepothilone b, cis`. The endostatin exact-ID control selects the intended CHEMBL4297946. The complete endostatin name's trial QA instead retrieves two CHEMBL4297747 rows.
 
-**Cause:** the first item contains an accepted drug; the next item contains only its qualifier. `src/query.py:192-197` saves this last item in `dangling` and `continue`s without emitting refusal. Transfer is exclusively to a later item (`183-184,199-201`); there is none, so the drug already found in the earlier item remains accepted. The accepted forward control `liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin` now refuses correctly. The forward-only repair therefore does not close the same single-product conjunction problem in the opposite order.
+This follows the unconditional item boundaries at `src/query.py:143,216-217,277`. Because the requested design explicitly imposes those boundaries, this is reported as an identity tradeoff/should-fix, not used as an additional approval blocker or mislabeled as an over-refusal. It needs an explicit user-visible limitation or a safe disambiguation policy. Do not bypass product guards merely because a full alias exists.
 
-**Scope qualification:** the full resolver also splits these phrases, accepts a parent component and refuses the trailing formulation component. This is a **shared parsing weakness**, not a map-versus-resolver contradiction. Unlike M6-B1, identification as a single-product description relies on the plain meaning of the complete phrase; the local whole-string predicate independently recognizes the qualifier. The definite M6-B1 contradiction alone already fails the approval bar. Required outcome: a qualifier stranded after a conjunction in a single-product description must not silently authorize its originator. Preserve ordinary separate-product controls such as `liposomal doxorubicin and osimertinib`.
+The same sweep records **57** formerly recognized lone aliases with no current Drug hit against the exact bf436c6 comparison: the accepted pegylated alias plus **56** punctuation/list-word cases such as `ICI 182,780`, `CP-675,206`, `Nk cells, autologous`, and `Anusol plus hc`. These deliberate boundary losses are recorded without treating intended over-refusal as a new blocker. None appears in the page demos. [summary](names/names_m5_summary.json).
 
-## Additional should-fix items and accepted tradeoffs
+Previously recorded lowercase-ID/name handling, blank submit, raw 400 details and Infinity behavior remain as itemized above. No new misleading cut notice, graph relation/direction, stored field value, or generated-markup injection defect was established in the exercised paths.
 
-1. **M6-S1 — new cross-item over-refusal of a parenthesized named product.** `(Mvasi) and bevacizumab` now yields only `refused:'(Mvasi) ... bevacizumab'` and no map/QA evidence; the exact M5 predecessor and current bare `Mvasi and bevacizumab` retain the independently listed BEVACIZUMAB. `[Mvasi]` and quoted Mvasi variants repeat it. `is_distinct_product` extracts brand words (`src/textrules.py:209-212`), but the `named` exception compares the raw lowercased token to brand sets (`src/query.py:188-191`). Parentheses turn a named product into a bare carried qualifier. This is outside the accepted within-item over-refusal: the valid drug is alone in the next item. [Focused comparison](names/focused_new.json).
-2. **M6-S2 — carry crosses a hard item boundary when a listing word is adjacent.** `liposomal, and doxorubicin`, `liposomal and; doxorubicin`, `liposomal and / doxorubicin` refuse DOXORUBICIN. `joined` checks only intervening `seg==-1` tokens (`src/query.py:183-184`), ignoring comma/slash clause changes. A comma-only `liposomal, doxorubicin` is separate. This is a new boundary over-refusal, not an originator leak. [Focused evidence](names/focused_new.json).
-3. **Measured alias consequences of the requested hard item rule — accepted design tradeoff.** Comma-containing unique stored names can stop linking or resolve only a fragment. `CP-358,774` now selects CHEMBL1079742 through `CP-358` rather than stored full alias CHEMBL553; `ENDOSTATIN, N-TERMINAL-MGGSHHHHH` selects ENDOSTATIN CHEMBL4297747 rather than CHEMBL4297946. These follow the explicit “names never span two items” policy, and are not counted as design violations or demo failures. The full alias scan and predecessor comparison retain the affected cases for review. [Alias scan](names/names_m5_summary.json), [focused comparison](names/focused_new.json).
-4. **Inherited Unicode guard/normalizer gap, also shared with the full resolver.** `ｂｅｖａｃｉｚｕｍａｂ－ａｗｗｂ`, `bevacizumab—awwb`, `bevacizumab‑awwb`, `[８９Ｚｒ] trastuzumab`, `［89Zr］ trastuzumab`, `[89Zr]—trastuzumab` accept originators in both revisions. Raw product/tracer guards return false; later alias keys normalize punctuation/width (`src/textrules.py:339-346`). ASCII suffix/tracer and en-dash suffix controls refuse. This extends the normalization coverage concern; it is not claimed to be introduced by this patch or a disagreement with current resolver output. [Exact cases](names/focused_new.json). Approval does not depend on this additional interpretation.
-5. M5-S2 remains the accepted pegylated-alias over-refusal. Lowercase IDs, Investigator-name lookup, blank submit, raw error details and Infinity remain the individually marked unchanged qualifications above.

+cases=[('plain_'+str(n),' '.join(['x']*n)) for n in (250,500,1000,2000)]

+"""Execute inherited reproducers, owning and closing one HTTP port at a time."""
+import json,os,subprocess,sys,time
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'root_checks'
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+env={**os.environ,'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1','PYTHONUTF8':'1','PYTHONIOENCODING':'utf-8',
+     'TMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TEMP':str(OUT/'tmp'),'TMPDIR':str(OUT/'tmp')}
+names=(['run_verification.py'] if 'tests' in sys.argv else ['perf_probe.py'] if 'perf' in sys.argv
+       else ['run_http.py','supplemental_http.py','attack_http.py','m5_http.py','cross_item_http.py','perf_check_http.py'])
+rows=[]
+for name in names:
+    start=time.perf_counter()
+    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-B',str(OUT/name)],cwd=ROOT,env=env,capture_output=True,timeout=240)
+    (OUT/(name+'.stdout.txt')).write_bytes(p.stdout+p.stderr)
+    rows.append({'script':name,'exit_code':p.returncode,'seconds':time.perf_counter()-start})
+    (OUT/('execution_'+sys.argv[-1]+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(rows[-1]),flush=True)
+    if p.returncode: raise SystemExit(p.returncode)

+**Cause:** `src/query.py:143-154` splits comma/semicolon and whitespace, strips `/` and `|` only from the edges of a token, and creates boundaries only for `startswith`/`endswith`. Interior `awwb/osimertinib` remains one token. `_qualified` (`99-110`) never checks a run ending at `awwb`, so the anchored suffix guard (`src/textrules.py:156-157,213-223`) misses it. The separate bare `bevacizumab` token is then accepted at `src/query.py:212-237`, and `Engine.explore` trusts it at `src/app.py:88-95`.

These controls isolate the missing **interior** boundary.

+### M6-B2 — P1: edge punctuation is removed for alias matching after defeating refusal
+
+Exact input **`bevacizumab-awwb。`**, with final **U+3002 IDEOGRAPHIC FULL STOP**, returns HTTP 200 and **BEVACIZUMAB / CHEMBL1201583**, **122 trials**, matched alias `Bevacizumab awwb`, and no refusal. The same `/api/resolve` input yields a single `bevacizumab-awwb: BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT` component with no accepted drug. [HTTP](root_checks/confirm_http.json).
+
+`Which trials evaluate bevacizumab-awwb。?` and `Which companies sponsor bevacizumab-awwb。?` each return **12 originator evidence rows**, with the same first trial/sponsor edges as B1. ASCII-period `bevacizumab-awwb.` and bare `bevacizumab-awwb` are refused; their trial/sponsor questions have **0 evidence**. Trailing U+2014, trailing ASCII hyphen and leading ASCII hyphen also produce the resolver/linker disagreement over HTTP.
+
+**Cause:** the strip set at `src/query.py:147` differs from the resolver's edge-punctuation normalization (`src/textrules.py:283,382-383`). The suffix regex is anchored at both ends (`156-157`), so the retained punctuation defeats it. The later `canonical_key` call (`src/query.py:221`; `src/textrules.py:339-346`) removes that punctuation and recovers an alias for the originator. Whole-item and eight-word checks preserve the same offending character, so increasing their coverage cannot fix this mismatch.
+
+**Status:** inherited under `514929c`, still a blocker. Quoted/bracketed suffix variants and some Unicode hyphens also fail both resolver and linker; those are explicitly distinguished from the exact U+3002 resolver disagreement in [independent findings](attacks/FINDINGS.md). No external pharmacology assumption is needed for the primary reproduction.
+
+**Required outcome:** normalization that enables a positive alias must not remove evidence that prevented its refusal guard from matching. The U+3002 and hyphen-edge controls must refuse the product before mapping or retrieving originator evidence.
+

+
+These are qualified descriptions of the preceding drug, not two separately named drugs. [Actual HTTP](root_checks/carry_http.json), [independent current/predecessor comparison](attacks/reversed_results.json).
+
+`Which trials evaluate doxorubicin (nanoparticle and liposomal formulation)?` returns **3** originator rows, first `NCT01970540|EVALUATES|CHEMBL53463|NCT01970540`. Its sponsor question returns 3 rows, first `ORG:imunon|SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF|CHEMBL53463|`. The corresponding two bevacizumab questions return **12** originator rows each, beginning with B1's trial/sponsor edges.
 
-## Approval blockers
+**Cause:** `src/query.py:167-174` splits on `and` even inside the parenthetical qualifier. The drug is in the earlier, unqualified item. The later `liposomal formulation)` or `biosimilar)` item has no drug, so `192-197` stores it as `dangling` and continues. Carry is applied only to a **subsequent** drug (`183-184,199-201`); it never retracts the already accepted earlier drug. The final qualifier can therefore disappear without even an output Refused entry. The full resolver preserves the original product context for its guard (`src/normalize.py:707,729-735`).
+
+Controls `doxorubicin (liposomal formulation)` and the original M5-B2 `liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin` both refuse and produce **0 QA evidence**. Separate-drug controls `osimertinib and liposomal doxorubicin` and `doxorubicin and liposomal paclitaxel` retain the independently named first drug. [HTTP controls](root_checks/carry_http.json).
 
-### M6-B1 — P1: an internal glued separator still hides a refused suffix
+**Status:** inherited under `514929c`; the latest forward-carry repair does not solve this design failure. The stated carry direction explains the implementation but does not waive the approval bar against a refused product becoming its originator. This is an **under-refusal**, not the accepted same-item over-refusal or M5-S2.
+
+**Required outcome:** preserve the qualifier's scope within an unambiguously single product description, or refuse that ambiguous product as a whole. Do not propagate every trailing qualifier backwards indiscriminately across genuinely separate named products.
+

-**Cause, with a discriminating control:** `src/query.py:143-154` splits commas and whitespace, strips `/|` only at token edges, and increments clauses only for starts/ends. It never splits internal `awwb/osimertinib`. Changing only separator placement changes safety. An eight-word window cannot repair a separator inside a token.

-The full-string `is_distinct_product` is true for both; each phrase names one drug in a qualified formulation. `and` joins formulation descriptions rather than two separately named drugs. Both are inherited in the exact M5-method comparator. [Direct predicate/comparison](names/focused_new.json), [actual HTTP, including sponsor QA](confirm_http.json).
+[HTTP and QA](root_checks/alias_http.json), [all six aliases, stored provenance and previous/current hits](names/names_m5_attack.json), [summary table](names/REPORT.md). The other examples are `Usp mab 002, monoclonal igg1`, `Usp mab 003, monoclonal igg1`, and `12,13-desoxyepothilone b, cis`. The endostatin exact-ID control selects the intended CHEMBL4297946. The complete endostatin name's trial QA instead retrieves two CHEMBL4297747 rows.
 
-**Cause:** the first item contains an accepted drug; the next item contains only its qualifier. `src/query.py:192-197` saves this last item in `dangling` and `continue`s without emitting refusal. Transfer is exclusively to a later item (`183-184,199-201`); there is none, so the drug already found in the earlier item remains accepted. The accepted forward control `liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin` now refuses correctly. The forward-only repair therefore does not close the same single-product conjunction problem in the opposite order.
+This follows the unconditional item boundaries at `src/query.py:143,216-217,277`. Because the requested design explicitly imposes those boundaries, this is reported as an identity tradeoff/should-fix, not used as an additional approval blocker or mislabeled as an over-refusal. It needs an explicit user-visible limitation or a safe disambiguation policy. Do not bypass product guards merely because a full alias exists.
 
-**Scope qualification:** the full resolver also splits these phrases, accepts a parent component and refuses the trailing formulation component. This is a **shared parsing weakness**, not a map-versus-resolver contradiction. Unlike M6-B1, identification as a single-product description relies on the plain meaning of the complete phrase; the local whole-string predicate independently recognizes the qualifier. The definite M6-B1 contradiction alone already fails the approval bar. Required outcome: a qualifier stranded after a conjunction in a single-product description must not silently authorize its originator. Preserve ordinary separate-product controls such as `liposomal doxorubicin and osimertinib`.
+The same sweep records **57** formerly recognized lone aliases with no current Drug hit against the exact bf436c6 comparison: the accepted pegylated alias plus **56** punctuation/list-word cases such as `ICI 182,780`, `CP-675,206`, `Nk cells, autologous`, and `Anusol plus hc`. These deliberate boundary losses are recorded without treating intended over-refusal as a new blocker. None appears in the page demos. [summary](names/names_m5_summary.json).
 
-## Additional should-fix items and accepted tradeoffs
+Previously recorded lowercase-ID/name handling, blank submit, raw 400 details and Infinity behavior remain as itemized above. No new misleading cut notice, graph relation/direction, stored field value, or generated-markup injection defect was established in the exercised paths.
 
-1. **M6-S1 — new cross-item over-refusal of a parenthesized named product.** `(Mvasi) and bevacizumab` now yields only `refused:'(Mvasi) ... bevacizumab'` and no map/QA evidence; the exact M5 predecessor and current bare `Mvasi and bevacizumab` retain the independently listed BEVACIZUMAB. `[Mvasi]` and quoted Mvasi variants repeat it. `is_distinct_product` extracts brand words (`src/textrules.py:209-212`), but the `named` exception compares the raw lowercased token to brand sets (`src/query.py:188-191`). Parentheses turn a named product into a bare carried qualifier. This is outside the accepted within-item over-refusal: the valid drug is alone in the next item. [Focused comparison](names/focused_new.json).
-2. **M6-S2 — carry crosses a hard item boundary when a listing word is adjacent.** `liposomal, and doxorubicin`, `liposomal and; doxorubicin`, `liposomal and / doxorubicin` refuse DOXORUBICIN. `joined` checks only intervening `seg==-1` tokens (`src/query.py:183-184`), ignoring comma/slash clause changes. A comma-only `liposomal, doxorubicin` is separate. This is a new boundary over-refusal, not an originator leak. [Focused evidence](names/focused_new.json).
-3. **Measured alias consequences of the requested hard item rule — accepted design tradeoff.** Comma-containing unique stored names can stop linking or resolve only a fragment. `CP-358,774` now selects CHEMBL1079742 through `CP-358` rather than stored full alias CHEMBL553; `ENDOSTATIN, N-TERMINAL-MGGSHHHHH` selects ENDOSTATIN CHEMBL4297747 rather than CHEMBL4297946. These follow the explicit “names never span two items” policy, and are not counted as design violations or demo failures. The full alias scan and predecessor comparison retain the affected cases for review. [Alias scan](names/names_m5_summary.json), [focused comparison](names/focused_new.json).
-4. **Inherited Unicode guard/normalizer gap, also shared with the full resolver.** `ｂｅｖａｃｉｚｕｍａｂ－ａｗｗｂ`, `bevacizumab—awwb`, `bevacizumab‑awwb`, `[８９Ｚｒ] trastuzumab`, `［89Zr］ trastuzumab`, `[89Zr]—trastuzumab` accept originators in both revisions. Raw product/tracer guards return false; later alias keys normalize punctuation/width (`src/textrules.py:339-346`). ASCII suffix/tracer and en-dash suffix controls refuse. This extends the normalization coverage concern; it is not claimed to be introduced by this patch or a disagreement with current resolver output. [Exact cases](names/focused_new.json). Approval does not depend on this additional interpretation.
-5. M5-S2 remains the accepted pegylated-alias over-refusal. Lowercase IDs, Investigator-name lookup, blank submit, raw error details and Infinity remain the individually marked unchanged qualifications above.

+"""End-to-end linker/check cost under HTTP's input and answer character caps."""
+import json,statistics,sys,time
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'root_checks'
+ROOT=OUT.parents[2]
+sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT/'src'))
+from app import Engine
+e=Engine();e.kg.conn.execute('PRAGMA query_only=ON')
+cases=[('plain_'+str(n),' '.join(['x']*n)) for n in (250,500,1000,2000)]
+cases += [('split_2000',';'.join(['x']*2000)),('many_matched',';'.join(['osimertinib']*333)),
+          ('many_refused',';'.join(['Mvasi bevacizumab']*222)),
+          ('many_joiners',' and '.join(['x']*666))]
+rows=[]
+for name,value in cases:
+    times=[]
+    for _ in range(3):
+        start=time.perf_counter(); ents=e.kg.link_entities(value); times.append(time.perf_counter()-start)
+    start=time.perf_counter(); result=e.check('EGFR',value); check=time.perf_counter()-start
+    rows.append({'name':name,'text':value,'characters':len(value),'link_seconds':times,
+                 'link_median_seconds':statistics.median(times),'check_seconds':check,
+                 'entity_count':len(ents),'evidence_count':result['evidence_count']})
+e.kg.conn.close()
+(OUT/'cost_stress.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps([{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k not in ('text','link_seconds')} for r in rows],indent=2))

+"""Verify every initially tracked byte and local evidence link without editing tracked files."""
+import hashlib,json,re,socket,subprocess
+from pathlib import Path
+OUT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[1]
+before=json.loads((OUT/'integrity_initial.json').read_text(encoding='utf8'))
+def git(*args):
+    p=subprocess.run(['git',*args],cwd=ROOT,capture_output=True)
+    return {'exit_code':p.returncode,'stdout':p.stdout.decode('utf8','replace'),
+            'stderr':p.stderr.decode('utf8','replace')}
+changed=[]
+for f,expected in before['tracked_sha256'].items():
+    p=ROOT/f
+    current=hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if p.is_file() else None
+    if current!=expected: changed.append({'file':f,'before':expected,'after':current})
+ports={}
+for port in (8896,8897,8899):
+    with socket.socket() as s: ports[str(port)]=s.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',port))!=0
+report=OUT/'REPORT.md'
+text=report.read_text(encoding='utf8')
+refs=re.findall(r'\[[^\]]+\]\(([^)]+)\)',text)
+missing=[]
+for ref in sorted(set(refs)):
+    if ref=='integrity_final.json': continue
+    if not (OUT/ref.split('#')[0]).is_file(): missing.append(ref)
+result={'head_before':before['head'],'head_after':git('rev-parse','HEAD'),
+        'tracked_files_compared':len(before['tracked_sha256']),'changed_tracked_files':changed,
+        'unstaged_diff':git('diff','--exit-code'),'staged_diff':git('diff','--cached','--exit-code'),
+        'db_sha256':hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'data/processed/oncokg.sqlite').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+        'owned_ports_closed':ports,'report_local_links_checked':len(set(refs)),
+        'missing_report_links':missing,'report_final_line':text.strip().splitlines()[-1],
+        'report_sha256':hashlib.sha256(report.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
+(OUT/'integrity_final.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf8')
+print(json.dumps(result,indent=2))
+assert not changed and not missing and all(ports.values())
+assert result['head_after']['stdout'].strip()==before['head']
+assert result['unstaged_diff']['exit_code']==result['staged_diff']['exit_code']==0
+assert result['report_final_line']=='VERDICT: CHANGES REQUIRED'
